# FileMan file #2 (PATIENT) to FHIR R4 Patient

Source data dictionary: `^DD(2,...)` from [WorldVistA/VistA-M Packages/VA FileMan/Globals/DD.zwr @ b7aecb9029f9](https://github.com/WorldVistA/VistA-M/blob/b7aecb9029f9bb8639a7bfa63b635469065ab44d/Packages/VA%20FileMan/Globals/DD.zwr). The `DD.zwr line` column is the line in that file where `^DD(<file>,<field>,0)` (the field definition node) sits; `FHIR/Patient/DD-2-extract.zwr` in this repo carries the same lines with the same numbers. `KIDS` lists local patch files in this repo (`Packages/.../*.KID[S]`) that also carry the field definition, as `path:line`.

Status legend: **mapped** = FHIR R4 Patient core element; **mapped-extension** = standard HL7 / US Core extension on Patient; **mapped-local-extension** = extension defined by this package because no standard one exists; **NO FHIR EQUIVALENT** = nothing on Patient; the `FHIR element / candidate` column says where the data would go instead.
